    - [38;2;177;185;249mROUTING_GRAPH.status: "DRAFT_VALIDATED_STRUCTURE"[m rests only on a self-check of node and edge counts.[K

  - [1m[97mP2-2: The rule compile copies [38;2;177;185;249mscope[97m text word for word,[m so it will drift when the registry changes. Store [38;2;177;185;249mrule_id[m plus the registry version and hash instead.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-3: Carry-over IDs don't trace back to the review file.[m [38;2;177;185;249mDEPLOYMENT[m and [38;2;177;185;249mHOSTED_RUNTIME_INTERCEPTION[m don't map to [38;2;177;185;249mSYSTEM_WIDE_REVIEW[m scope IDs [38;2;177;185;249mdeployment_netlify[m and [38;2;177;185;249mhosted_runtime_interception[m.  
    The state also changed from [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED[m to [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED_CONTAINED[m; that change came from the manifest, but it isn't cross-referenced. Add [38;2;177;185;249msource_scope_id[m.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-4: Frozen Beta files point to paths that don't exist.[m The manifest's [38;2;177;185;249madversarial_closure[m and the readiness file's [38;2;177;185;249mmanifest[m field point to [38;2;177;185;249mBETA_REVIEW/…20261003.json[m. Those files are in[K
    [38;2;177;185;249mALPHA_INPUT/FROZEN_BETA/[m. The frozen files can't be edited, so record the remapping in the input lock.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-5: Some Beta domains are mentioned but never structured.[m These appear only in the rule list, not as routing nodes or contracts:[K
    - the enforcement engine and executor lifecycle ([38;2;177;185;249mENFORCEMENT/execution_state_engine.py[m, TKY-CODEX-001)[K

[K
  - [1m[97mP2-1: Some validation results claim more than was checked.[m[K
    - [38;2;177;185;249mRULE_COMPILE.status: "PASS"[m only proves owners exist and the copy is 1:1. MASTER §18 A2 says it should map rules to executable gates, which wasn't done.[K
    - [38;2;177;185;249mROUTING_GRAPH.status: "DRAFT_VALIDATED_STRUCTURE"[m rests only on a self-check of node and edge counts.[K
    - [1m[97mFix:[m use [38;2;177;185;249mOWNER_EXISTENCE_PASS / GATE_MAPPING_NOT_RUN[m and [38;2;177;185;249mDRAFT_SELF_COUNTED[m.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-2: The rule compile copies [38;2;177;185;249mscope[97m text word for word,[m so it will drift when the registry changes. Store [38;2;177;185;249mrule_id[m plus the registry version and hash instead.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-3: Carry-over IDs don't trace back to the review file.[m [38;2;177;185;249mDEPLOYMENT[m and [38;2;177;185;249mHOSTED_RUNTIME_INTERCEPTION[m don't map to [38;2;177;185;249mSYSTEM_WIDE_REVIEW[m scope IDs [38;2;177;185;249mdeployment_netlify[m and [38;2;177;185;249mhosted_runtime_interception[m.  
    The state also changed from [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED[m to [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED_CONTAINED[m; that change came from the manifest, but it isn't cross-referenced. Add [38;2;177;185;249msource_scope_id[m.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-4: Frozen Beta files point to paths that don't exist.[m The manifest's [38;2;177;185;249madversarial_closure[m and the readiness file's [38;2;177;185;249mmanifest[m field point to [38;2;177;185;249mBETA_REVIEW/…20261003.json[m. Those files are in[K
    [38;2;177;185;249mALPHA_INPUT/FROZEN_BETA/[m. The frozen files can't be edited, so record the remapping in the input lock.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-5: Some Beta domains are mentioned but never structured.[m These appear only in the rule list, not as routing nodes or contracts:[K
    - the enforcement engine and executor lifecycle ([38;2;177;185;249mENFORCEMENT/execution_state_engine.py[m, TKY-CODEX-001)[K
    - the Notion / Drive C2S workspace (TKY-WORKSPACE / NOTION / DRIVEC2S)[K
    - handoff / checkpoint[K
[K
    Separately, Beta itself has a gap: [38;2;177;185;249mREVISION_GOVERNANCE.md[m, [38;2;177;185;249mTRACEABILITY_PROTOCOL.md[m and [38;2;177;185;249mCONVERSATION_CONTEXT_LEDGER_PROTOCOL.md[m are marked ACTIVE_OWNER but have no rule ID. Carry that over as an  
    OPEN item.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-6: The "V0_1" version label sits awkwardly with MASTER_LOGIC §13,[m which says everything is REV_00 until a user finalizes it. Note that "V0.1" is a draft label, not a Revision.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-7: MASTER_LOGIC §21 requires an old→new migration and decision-coverage matrix before canonical replacement,[m and Alpha has none. Add it to the A7 trace gate.[K

  [1m[97mP2[m[K
[K
  - [1m[97mP2-1: Some validation results claim more than was checked.[m[K
    - [38;2;177;185;249mRULE_COMPILE.status: "PASS"[m only proves owners exist and the copy is 1:1. MASTER §18 A2 says it should map rules to executable gates, which wasn't done.[K
    - [38;2;177;185;249mROUTING_GRAPH.status: "DRAFT_VALIDATED_STRUCTURE"[m rests only on a self-check of node and edge counts.[K
    - [1m[97mFix:[m use [38;2;177;185;249mOWNER_EXISTENCE_PASS / GATE_MAPPING_NOT_RUN[m and [38;2;177;185;249mDRAFT_SELF_COUNTED[m.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-2: The rule compile copies [38;2;177;185;249mscope[97m text word for word,[m so it will drift when the registry changes. Store [38;2;177;185;249mrule_id[m plus the registry version and hash instead.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-3: Carry-over IDs don't trace back to the review file.[m [38;2;177;185;249mDEPLOYMENT[m and [38;2;177;185;249mHOSTED_RUNTIME_INTERCEPTION[m don't map to [38;2;177;185;249mSYSTEM_WIDE_REVIEW[m scope IDs [38;2;177;185;249mdeployment_netlify[m and [38;2;177;185;249mhosted_runtime_interception[m.  
    The state also changed from [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED[m to [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED_CONTAINED[m; that change came from the manifest, but it isn't cross-referenced. Add [38;2;177;185;249msource_scope_id[m.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-4: Frozen Beta files point to paths that don't exist.[m The manifest's [38;2;177;185;249madversarial_closure[m and the readiness file's [38;2;177;185;249mmanifest[m field point to [38;2;177;185;249mBETA_REVIEW/…20261003.json[m. Those files are in[K
    [38;2;177;185;249mALPHA_INPUT/FROZEN_BETA/[m. The frozen files can't be edited, so record the remapping in the input lock.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-5: Some Beta domains are mentioned but never structured.[m These appear only in the rule list, not as routing nodes or contracts:[K
    - the enforcement engine and executor lifecycle ([38;2;177;185;249mENFORCEMENT/execution_state_engine.py[m, TKY-CODEX-001)[K
    - the Notion / Drive C2S workspace (TKY-WORKSPACE / NOTION / DRIVEC2S)[K
    - handoff / checkpoint[K
[K
    Separately, Beta itself has a gap: [38;2;177;185;249mREVISION_GOVERNANCE.md[m, [38;2;177;185;249mTRACEABILITY_PROTOCOL.md[m and [38;2;177;185;249mCONVERSATION_CONTEXT_LEDGER_PROTOCOL.md[m are marked ACTIVE_OWNER but have no rule ID. Carry that over as an  
    OPEN item.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-6: The "V0_1" version label sits awkwardly with MASTER_LOGIC §13,[m which says everything is REV_00 until a user finalizes it. Note that "V0.1" is a draft label, not a Revision.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-7: MASTER_LOGIC §21 requires an old→new migration and decision-coverage matrix before canonical replacement,[m and Alpha has none. Add it to the A7 trace gate.[K

  - [1m[97mP2-3: Carry-over IDs don't trace back to the review file.[m [38;2;177;185;249mDEPLOYMENT[m and [38;2;177;185;249mHOSTED_RUNTIME_INTERCEPTION[m don't map to [38;2;177;185;249mSYSTEM_WIDE_REVIEW[m scope IDs [38;2;177;185;249mdeployment_netlify[m and [38;2;177;185;249mhosted_runtime_interception[m.

    - [38;2;177;185;249mROUTING_GRAPH.status: "DRAFT_VALIDATED_STRUCTURE"[m rests only on a self-check of node and edge counts.[K
    - [1m[97mFix:[m use [38;2;177;185;249mOWNER_EXISTENCE_PASS / GATE_MAPPING_NOT_RUN[m and [38;2;177;185;249mDRAFT_SELF_COUNTED[m.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-2: The rule compile copies [38;2;177;185;249mscope[97m text word for word,[m so it will drift when the registry changes. Store [38;2;177;185;249mrule_id[m plus the registry version and hash instead.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-3: Carry-over IDs don't trace back to the review file.[m [38;2;177;185;249mDEPLOYMENT[m and [38;2;177;185;249mHOSTED_RUNTIME_INTERCEPTION[m don't map to [38;2;177;185;249mSYSTEM_WIDE_REVIEW[m scope IDs [38;2;177;185;249mdeployment_netlify[m and [38;2;177;185;249mhosted_runtime_interception[m.  
    The state also changed from [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED[m to [38;2;177;185;249mUNVERIFIED_CONTAINED[m; that change came from the manifest, but it isn't cross-referenced. Add [38;2;177;185;249msource_scope_id[m.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-4: Frozen Beta files point to paths that don't exist.[m The manifest's [38;2;177;185;249madversarial_closure[m and the readiness file's [38;2;177;185;249mmanifest[m field point to [38;2;177;185;249mBETA_REVIEW/…20261003.json[m. Those files are in[K
    [38;2;177;185;249mALPHA_INPUT/FROZEN_BETA/[m. The frozen files can't be edited, so record the remapping in the input lock.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-5: Some Beta domains are mentioned but never structured.[m These appear only in the rule list, not as routing nodes or contracts:[K
    - the enforcement engine and executor lifecycle ([38;2;177;185;249mENFORCEMENT/execution_state_engine.py[m, TKY-CODEX-001)[K
    - the Notion / Drive C2S workspace (TKY-WORKSPACE / NOTION / DRIVEC2S)[K
    - handoff / checkpoint[K
[K
    Separately, Beta itself has a gap: [38;2;177;185;249mREVISION_GOVERNANCE.md[m, [38;2;177;185;249mTRACEABILITY_PROTOCOL.md[m and [38;2;177;185;249mCONVERSATION_CONTEXT_LEDGER_PROTOCOL.md[m are marked ACTIVE_OWNER but have no rule ID. Carry that over as an  
    OPEN item.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-6: The "V0_1" version label sits awkwardly with MASTER_LOGIC §13,[m which says everything is REV_00 until a user finalizes it. Note that "V0.1" is a draft label, not a Revision.[K
  - [1m[97mP2-7: MASTER_LOGIC §21 requires an old→new migration and decision-coverage matrix before canonical replacement,[m and Alpha has none. Add it to the A7 trace gate.[K
